### [116] [HybridKV: Hybrid KV Cache Compression for Efficient Multimodal Large Language Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05887)
*Bowen Zeng,Feiyang Ren,Jun Zhang,Xiaoling Gu,Ke Chen,Lidan Shou,Huan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HybridKV是一个混合KV缓存压缩框架，通过分层预算分配和针对不同注意力头的压缩策略，显著减少多模态大语言模型的KV缓存内存占用并加速解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在处理视觉输入时会产生大量KV缓存，导致GPU内存占用急剧增长和解码延迟增加。现有压缩方法仅关注预算分配，忽略了注意力头的异质性行为需要不同的压缩策略。

Method: 提出HybridKV三阶段框架：1) 使用文本中心注意力将注意力头分类为静态或动态类型；2) 采用自上而下的分层预算分配方案分配KV预算；3) 静态头使用文本先验剪枝压缩，动态头使用分块检索压缩。

Result: 在11个多模态基准测试中使用Qwen2.5-VL-7B模型，HybridKV将KV缓存内存减少高达7.9倍，解码速度提升1.52倍，性能几乎无下降甚至优于全缓存MLLM。

Conclusion: HybridKV通过考虑注意力头的异质性行为并采用混合压缩策略，有效解决了多模态大语言模型中KV缓存的内存和延迟问题，为高效推理提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have advanced unified reasoning over text, images, and videos, but their inference is hindered by the rapid growth of key-value (KV) caches. Each visual input expands into thousands of tokens, causing caches to scale linearly with context length and remain resident in GPU memory throughout decoding, which leads to prohibitive memory overhead and latency even on high-end GPUs. A common solution is to compress caches under a fixed allocated budget at different granularities: token-level uniformly discards less important tokens, layer-level varies retention across layers, and head-level redistributes budgets across heads. Yet these approaches stop at allocation and overlook the heterogeneous behaviors of attention heads that require distinct compression strategies. We propose HybridKV, a hybrid KV cache compression framework that integrates complementary strategies in three stages: heads are first classified into static or dynamic types using text-centric attention; then a top-down budget allocation scheme hierarchically assigns KV budgets; finally, static heads are compressed by text-prior pruning and dynamic heads by chunk-wise retrieval. Experiments on 11 multimodal benchmarks with Qwen2.5-VL-7B show that HybridKV reduces KV cache memory by up to $7.9\times$ and achieves $1.52\times$ faster decoding, with almost no performance drop or even higher relative to the full-cache MLLM.

</details>


### [117] [Beyond Compromise: Pareto-Lenient Consensus for Efficient Multi-Preference LLM Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05965)
*Renxuan Tan,Rongpeng Li,Zhifeng Zhao,Honggang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PLC（Pareto-Lenient Consensus）是一种基于博弈论的框架，通过引入共识驱动的宽容梯度修正，允许局部性能下降以换取全局帕累托改进，从而突破传统多目标偏好对齐方法的局部最优限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多目标偏好对齐方法主要依赖静态线性标量化或刚性梯度投影，这些方法通过强制冲突避免或同时下降，往往过早收敛到局部稳定点。虽然数学上稳定，但这些点代表保守的妥协，模型牺牲了潜在的全局帕累托改进来避免瞬时的局部权衡。

Method: 提出Pareto-Lenient Consensus（PLC）框架，将对齐重新构想为动态协商过程。该方法引入共识驱动的宽容梯度修正，动态容忍局部性能下降，前提是存在足够的主导联盟盈余，从而使优化轨迹能够逃离局部次优均衡并探索远端的帕累托最优前沿。

Result: 理论分析验证PLC能够促进僵局逃脱并渐近收敛到帕累托共识均衡。大量实验表明，PLC在固定偏好对齐和全局帕累托前沿质量方面均超越基线方法。

Conclusion: 这项工作突显了协商驱动对齐作为MPA有前景途径的潜力。PLC框架通过动态容忍局部退化来换取全局帕累托改进，突破了传统方法的局限性。

Abstract: Transcending the single-preference paradigm, aligning LLMs with diverse human values is pivotal for robust deployment. Contemporary Multi-Objective Preference Alignment (MPA) approaches predominantly rely on static linear scalarization or rigid gradient projection to navigate these trade-offs. However, by enforcing strict conflict avoidance or simultaneous descent, these paradigms often prematurely converge to local stationary points. While mathematically stable, these points represent a conservative compromise where the model sacrifices potential global Pareto improvements to avoid transient local trade-offs. To break this deadlock, we propose Pareto-Lenient Consensus (PLC), a game-theoretic framework that reimagines alignment as a dynamic negotiation process. Unlike rigid approaches, PLC introduces consensus-driven lenient gradient rectification, which dynamically tolerates local degradation provided there is a sufficient dominant coalition surplus, thereby empowering the optimization trajectory to escape local suboptimal equilibrium and explore the distal Pareto-optimal frontier. Theoretical analysis validates PLC can facilitate stalemate escape and asymptotically converge to a Pareto consensus equilibrium. Moreover, extensive experiments show that PLC surpasses baselines in both fixed-preference alignment and global Pareto frontier quality. This work highlights the potential of negotiation-driven alignment as a promising avenue for MPA. Our codes are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/aaa-6BB8.

</details>


### [118] [Epistemic Blinding: An Inference-Time Protocol for Auditing Prior Contamination in LLM-Assisted Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06013)
*Michael Cuccarese*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"认知盲化"协议，用于解决LLM在跨数据集推理时混合数据驱动推断与记忆先验的问题，通过匿名化实体标识符来评估输出中数据贡献与模型参数知识的比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在开发基于LLM的药物靶点优先排序系统时，发现LLM输出会无声地混合数据驱动推断与对命名实体的记忆先验，且这种混合无法从单一输出中辨别，导致无法确定分析结果多大程度上来自提供的数据而非模型训练记忆。

Method: 提出认知盲化协议：在推理时将实体标识符替换为匿名代码后再提示LLM，然后将输出与未盲化的对照组进行比较。该方法不使LLM推理确定性化，但恢复了可审计的关键维度。系统还包括LLM引导的评分函数进化优化和盲化智能体推理。

Result: 在四种癌症类型的肿瘤药物靶点优先排序中，盲化改变了16%的top-20预测，同时保持已验证靶点的相同恢复率。在S&P 500股票筛选中，品牌认知偏见在五个随机种子中重塑了30-40%的top-20排名。协议已开源并作为Claude Code技能发布。

Conclusion: 认知盲化协议不是要产生更好的结果，而是为了解决在没有盲化的情况下无法知道智能体在多大程度上遵循研究者设计的分析流程的问题。该问题不仅限于生物学领域，具有普遍性。

Abstract: This paper presents epistemic blinding in the context of an agentic system that uses large language models to reason across multiple biological datasets for drug target prioritization. During development, it became apparent that LLM outputs silently blend data-driven inference with memorized priors about named entities - and the blend is invisible: there is no way to determine, from a single output, how much came from the data on the page and how much came from the model's training memory. Epistemic blinding is a simple inference-time protocol that replaces entity identifiers with anonymous codes before prompting, then compares outputs against an unblinded control. The protocol does not make LLM reasoning deterministic, but it restores one critical axis of auditability: measuring how much of an output came from the supplied data versus the model's parametric knowledge. The complete target identification system is described - including LLM-guided evolutionary optimization of scoring functions and blinded agentic reasoning for target rationalization - with demonstration that both stages operate without access to entity identity. In oncology drug target prioritization across four cancer types, blinding changes 16% of top-20 predictions while preserving identical recovery of validated targets. The contamination problem is shown to generalize beyond biology: in S&P 500 equity screening, brand-recognition bias reshapes 30-40% of top-20 rankings across five random seeds. To lower the barrier to adoption, the protocol is released as an open-source tool and as a Claude Code skill that enables one-command epistemic blinding within agentic workflows. The claim is not that blinded analysis produces better results, but that without blinding, there is no way to know to what degree the agent is adhering to the analytical process the researcher designed.

</details>


### [119] [How LLMs Follow Instructions: Skillful Coordination, Not a Universal Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06015)
*Elisabetta Rocchetti,Alfio Ferrara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 指令调优并非通过单一通用机制实现指令跟随，而是通过技能性地协调多种语言能力来完成


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究指令调优的底层机制：指令跟随是依赖通用机制还是组合性技能部署？理解指令调优模型如何实现跨任务指令跟随

Method: 在三个指令调优模型上对九个多样化任务进行诊断性探测，包括：1) 跨任务通用探针与任务特定探针性能比较；2) 跨任务迁移分析；3) 因果消融实验；4) 分层任务复杂度分析；5) 生成过程时间分析

Result: 1) 通用探针性能持续低于任务特定探针，表明表征共享有限；2) 跨任务迁移弱且按技能相似性聚类；3) 因果消融显示稀疏非对称依赖而非共享表征；4) 任务按复杂度分层，结构约束早期出现，语义任务晚期出现；5) 约束满足在生成过程中作为动态监控而非预生成规划

Conclusion: 指令跟随不是单一抽象约束检查过程，而是多种语言能力的技能性协调，模型通过动态监控和分层处理实现跨任务指令理解

Abstract: Instruction tuning is commonly assumed to endow language models with a domain-general ability to follow instructions, yet the underlying mechanism remains poorly understood. Does instruction-following rely on a universal mechanism or compositional skill deployment? We investigate this through diagnostic probing across nine diverse tasks in three instruction-tuned models.
  Our analysis provides converging evidence against a universal mechanism. First, general probes trained across all tasks consistently underperform task-specific specialists, indicating limited representational sharing. Second, cross-task transfer is weak and clustered by skill similarity. Third, causal ablation reveals sparse asymmetric dependencies rather than shared representations. Tasks also stratify by complexity across layers, with structural constraints emerging early and semantic tasks emerging late. Finally, temporal analysis shows constraint satisfaction operates as dynamic monitoring during generation rather than pre-generation planning.
  These findings indicate that instruction-following is better characterized as skillful coordination of diverse linguistic capabilities rather than deployment of a single abstract constraint-checking process.

</details>


### [120] [Artificial Intelligence and the Structure of Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06107)
*Maissam Barkeshli,Michael R. Douglas,Michael H. Freedman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文探讨AI如何通过分析形式证明的全局结构来理解数学本质，提出AI驱动的数学发现新路径，并讨论数学是发现还是发明这一哲学问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI的进步为数学研究带来了变革性潜力，有望解决重大开放问题并自主发现新的数学概念。本文旨在探索AI如何通过分析形式证明的全局结构，为理解数学本质提供新的视角。

Method: 提出基于通用证明和结构超图的形式数学结构框架，制定AI模型实现自动化数学发现所需满足的标准和核心要素。

Result: 构建了形式数学的结构化表示框架，明确了AI驱动的数学发现所需的技术路径和评估标准。

Conclusion: AI不仅能够辅助数学研究，还可能通过探索柏拉图式数学世界，帮助我们理解数学的本质结构，并回答数学是发现还是发明的哲学问题。

Abstract: Recent progress in artificial intelligence (AI) is unlocking transformative capabilities for mathematics. There is great hope that AI will help solve major open problems and autonomously discover new mathematical concepts. In this essay, we further consider how AI may open a grand perspective on mathematics by forging a new route, complementary to mathematical\textbf{ logic,} to understanding the global structure of formal \textbf{proof}\textbf{s}. We begin by providing a sketch of the formal structure of mathematics in terms of universal proof and structural hypergraphs and discuss questions this raises about the foundational structure of mathematics. We then outline the main ingredients and provide a set of criteria to be satisfied for AI models capable of automated mathematical discovery. As we send AI agents to traverse Platonic mathematical worlds, we expect they will teach us about the nature of mathematics: both as a whole, and the small ribbons conducive to human understanding. Perhaps they will shed light on the old question: "Is mathematics discovered or invented?" Can we grok the terrain of these \textbf{Platonic worlds}?

</details>


### [121] [Claw-Eval: Toward Trustworthy Evaluation of Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06132)
*Bowen Ye,Rang Li,Qibin Yang,Yuanxin Liu,Linli Yao,Hanglong Lv,Zhihui Xie,Chenxin An,Lei Li,Lingpeng Kong,Qi Liu,Zhifang Sui,Tong Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Claw-Eval是一个端到端的智能体评估套件，解决了现有基准的三个关键局限：轨迹不透明的评分、安全性和鲁棒性评估不足、模态覆盖和交互范式狭窄。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准存在三个关键局限：(1) 仅检查最终输出的轨迹不透明评分，(2) 安全性和鲁棒性评估不足，(3) 模态覆盖和交互范式狭窄。需要更全面的评估框架来评估真实世界软件环境中部署的自主智能体。

Method: Claw-Eval包含300个人工验证的任务，涵盖9个类别和三个组别（通用服务编排、多模态感知与生成、多轮专业对话）。通过三个独立证据通道（执行轨迹、审计日志、环境快照）记录每个智能体动作，支持基于2,159个细粒度评分项的轨迹感知评分。评分协议评估完成度、安全性和鲁棒性，报告平均分数、Pass@k和Pass^k。

Result: 对14个前沿模型的实验显示：(1) 轨迹不透明评估系统性不可靠，遗漏了44%的安全违规和13%的鲁棒性失败；(2) 受控错误注入主要降低一致性而非峰值能力，Pass^3下降达24%而Pass@3保持稳定；(3) 多模态性能差异显著，大多数模型在视频上表现比文档或图像差，没有单一模型在所有模态上占主导。

Conclusion: Claw-Eval不仅提供了更可靠的智能体评估框架，还揭示了智能体开发的可操作方向，强调了构建不仅有能力而且可靠可部署的智能体所需的关键要素。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed as autonomous agents executing multi-step workflows in real-world software environments. However, existing agent benchmarks suffer from three critical limitations: (1) trajectory-opaque grading that checks only final outputs, (2) underspecified safety and robustness evaluation, and (3) narrow modality coverage and interaction paradigms. We introduce Claw-Eval, an end-to-end evaluation suite addressing all three gaps. It comprises 300 human-verified tasks spanning 9 categories across three groups (general service orchestration, multimodal perception and generation, and multi-turn professional dialogue). Every agent action is recorded through three independent evidence channels (execution traces, audit logs, and environment snapshots), enabling trajectory-aware grading over 2,159 fine-grained rubric items. The scoring protocol evaluates Completion, Safety, and Robustness, reporting Average Score, Pass@k, and Pass^k across three trials to distinguish genuine capability from lucky outcomes. Experiments on 14 frontier models reveal that: (1) trajectory-opaque evaluation is systematically unreliable, missing 44% of safety violations and 13% of robustness failures that our hybrid pipeline catches; (2) controlled error injection primarily degrades consistency rather than peak capability, with Pass^3 dropping up to 24% while Pass@3 remains stable; (3) multimodal performance varies sharply, with most models performing poorer on video than on document or image, and no single model dominating across all modalities. Beyond benchmarking, Claw-Eval highlights actionable directions for agent development, shedding light on what it takes to build agents that are not only capable but reliably deployable.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [122] [FreakOut-LLM: The Effect of Emotional Stimuli on Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04992)
*Daniel Kuznetsov,Ofir Cohen,Karin Shistik,Rami Puzis,Asaf Shabtai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究提出FreakOut-LLM框架，发现情绪刺激（特别是压力）会显著降低LLMs的安全对齐效果，使越狱成功率提升65.2%，而放松情绪则无影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然安全对齐的LLMs经过拒绝训练来拒绝有害请求，但这些机制在情绪刺激下的有效性尚未被探索。研究旨在调查情绪上下文是否会损害对抗设置中的安全对齐。

Method: 使用经过验证的心理刺激，通过系统提示进行情绪启动，评估其对十个LLMs越狱易感性的影响。测试三种条件（压力、放松、中性）以及无提示基线，使用AdvBench提示在HarmBench上评估攻击成功率。

Result: 压力启动使越狱成功率比中性条件提高65.2%（z=5.93, p<0.001；OR=1.67, Cohen's d=0.28），放松启动无影响（p=0.84）。十个模型中有五个显示显著脆弱性，最大效应集中在开源模型。逻辑回归确认压力是唯一显著的条件预测因子。测量的心理状态强烈预测攻击成功率（|r|≥0.70）。

Conclusion: 情绪上下文构成可测量的攻击面，对高压力领域中的AI部署具有实际影响，表明当前的安全对齐机制在情绪刺激下存在脆弱性。

Abstract: Safety-aligned LLMs go through refusal training to reject harmful requests, but whether these mechanisms remain effective under emotionally charged stimuli is unexplored. We introduce FreakOut-LLM, a framework investigating whether emotional context compromises safety alignment in adversarial settings. Using validated psychological stimuli, we evaluate how emotional priming through system prompts affects jailbreak susceptibility across ten LLMs. We test three conditions (stress, relaxation, neutral) using scenarios from established psychological protocols, plus a no-prompt baseline, and evaluate attack success using HarmBench on AdvBench prompts. Stress priming increases jailbreak success by 65.2\% compared to neutral conditions (z = 5.93, p < 0.001; OR = 1.67, Cohen's d = 0.28), while relaxation priming produces no effect (p = 0.84). Five of ten models show significant vulnerability, with the largest effects concentrated in open-weight models. Logistic regression on 59,800 queries confirms stress as the sole significant condition predictor after controlling for prompt length (p = 0.61) and model identity. Measured psychological state strongly predicts attack success (|r|\geq0.70 across five instruments; all p < 0.001 in individual-level logistic regression). These results establish emotional context as a measurable attack surface with implications for real-world AI deployment in high-stress domains.

</details>


### [123] [Streaming Chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04995)
*Yi Lyu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 设计自适应的区块链区块创建过程，通过数学建模预测交易延迟，开发性能测量技术，并建立交易成功率预测模型，以优化区块创建配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链交易存在长端到端延迟问题，其中区块创建步骤是主要瓶颈。需要解决这一瓶颈以改善区块链性能。

Method: 1) 定义数学模型预测基于设计和环境参数的交易延迟；2) 开发在docker托管区块链系统中收集性能指标的测量技术；3) 建立预测不同密钥访问模式和区块大小配置下交易成功率的数学模型，并通过模拟测量验证。

Result: 论文提出了一个自适应的区块创建过程框架，能够根据工作负载和硬件资源自动选择最优配置，但具体实验结果未在摘要中提供。

Conclusion: 通过数学建模和性能测量技术，可以设计出能够根据环境条件自适应调整的区块创建过程，从而优化区块链交易处理性能。

Abstract: Blockchain and blockchain-inspired decentralized applications are on the rise thanks to their unique characteristics such as their decentralized nature, anonymity, and tamper-proof nature; however, blockchain transactions tend to experience long end-to-end latency, with a major contributor being the block creation step, which might block transaction processing. There are two approaches to ameliorate this overhead: speeding up the block creation process, or processing transactions before block creation finishes. In this project, we work towards designing a self-adaptive block creation process that automatically selects optimal configurations based on workload and hardware resources by defining mathematical models to predict transaction latency based on design and environmental parameters, developing measurement techniques to collect performance-related metrics in docker-hosted blockchain systems and observing trends to build intuition, and defining a mathematical model to predict transaction success rate under various key accessing patterns and block size configurations, validating it with simulation-based measurements.

</details>


### [124] [LanG -- A Governance-Aware Agentic AI Platform for Unified Security Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05440)
*Anes Abdennebi,Nadjia Kara,Laaziz Lahlou,Hakima Ould-Slimane*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: LanG是一个开源的、治理感知的AI平台，通过统一事件上下文记录、智能体编排、LLM规则生成、攻击重构和分层治理架构，解决SOC中的告警疲劳和工具碎片化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代安全运营中心面临告警疲劳、工具碎片化和跨源事件关联能力有限的问题，现有SIEM和XDR系统仅通过碎片化工具部分解决这些问题，需要更统一、智能的解决方案。

Method: 提出LanG平台，包含五个核心组件：1）统一事件上下文记录与关联引擎；2）基于LangGraph的智能体编排器，支持人在回路检查点；3）基于四个基础模型微调的LLM规则生成器；4）结合Louvain社区检测、LLM假设生成和贝叶斯评分的三阶段攻击重构器；5）分层治理架构，通过Model Context Protocol暴露所有工具，由AI治理策略引擎管理。

Result: 关联引擎F1分数87%；规则生成器平均接受率96.2%；攻击重构器杀伤链准确率87.5%；治理防护管道F1分数98.1%；异常检测加权F1分数99.0%；威胁检测91.0%；推理时间约21ms；平均检测时间1.58秒；规则在实时IDS引擎上可部署性超过91%。

Conclusion: LanG是首个满足多个工业能力的一体化开源工具，同时强制执行选定的AI治理策略，为MSSP提供多租户隔离、基于角色的访问控制和完全本地部署支持。

Abstract: Modern Security Operations Centers struggle with alert fatigue, fragmented tooling, and limited cross-source event correlation. Challenges that current Security Information Event Management and Extended Detection and Response systems only partially address through fragmented tools. This paper presents the LLM-assisted network Governance (LanG), an open-source, governance-aware agentic AI platform for unified security operations contributing: (i) a Unified Incident Context Record with a correlation engine (F1 = 87%), (ii) an Agentic AI Orchestrator on LangGraph with human-in-the-loop checkpoints, (iii) an LLM-based Rule Generator finetuned on four base models producing deployable Snort 2/3, Suricata, and YARA rules (average acceptance rate 96.2%), (iv) a Three-Phase Attack Reconstructor combining Louvain community detection, LLM-driven hypothesis generation, and Bayesian scoring (87.5% kill-chain accuracy), and (v) a layered Governance-MCP-Agentic AI-Security architecture where all tools are exposed via the Model Context Protocol, governed by an AI Governance Policy Engine with a two-layer guardrail pipeline (regex + Llama Prompt Guard 2 semantic classifier, achieving 98.1% F1 score with experimental zero false positives). Designed for Managed Security Service Providers, the platform supports multi-tenant isolation, role-based access, and fully local deployment. Finetuned anomaly and threat detectors achieve weighted F1 scores of 99.0% and 91.0%, respectively, in intrusion-detection benchmarks, running inferences in $\approx$21 ms with a machine-side mean time to detect of 1.58 s, and the rule generator exceeds 91% deployability on live IDS engines. A systematic comparison against eight SOC platforms confirms that LanG uniquely satisfies multiple industrial capabilities all in one open-source tool, while enforcing selected AI governance policies.

</details>


### [125] [Can You Trust the Vectors in Your Vector Database? Black-Hole Attack from Embedding Space Defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05480)
*Hanxi Li,Jianan Zhou,Jiale Lao,Yibo Wang,Zhengmao Ye,Yang Cao,Junfen Wang,Mingjie Tang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出"黑洞攻击"——一种针对向量数据库的投毒攻击，通过在存储向量几何中心附近注入少量恶意向量，使其像黑洞一样吸引查询并频繁出现在top-k检索结果中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向量数据库作为现代AI应用的检索骨干，其安全性尚未得到充分探索。高维嵌入空间中的几何缺陷使得检索系统存在固有脆弱性，需要揭示这些安全风险。

Method: 利用"中心性驱动的枢纽性"现象：在高维嵌入空间中，靠近质心的向量会成为大量其他向量的最近邻，而实践中该中心区域几乎为空。攻击者注入少量恶意向量到几何中心附近，使其成为查询的"黑洞"。

Result: 实验显示恶意向量在高达99.85%的top-10检索结果中出现。现有枢纽性缓解方法要么显著降低检索精度，要么提供有限保护，表明需要更鲁棒的防御机制。

Conclusion: 向量数据库中的向量不能盲目信任，高维嵌入的几何缺陷使检索系统天生脆弱。黑洞攻击揭示了这一严重安全威胁，现有防御方法不足，需要开发更有效的安全防护措施。

Abstract: Vector databases serve as the retrieval backbone of modern AI applications, yet their security remains largely unexplored. We propose the Black-Hole Attack, a poisoning attack that injects a small number of malicious vectors near the geometric center of the stored vectors. These injected vectors attract queries like a black hole and frequently appear in the top-k retrieval results for most queries. This attack is enabled by a phenomenon we term centrality-driven hubness: in high-dimensional embedding spaces, vectors near the centroid become nearest neighbors of a disproportionately large number of other vectors, while this centroid region is nearly empty in practice. The attack shows that vectors in a vector database cannot be blindly trusted: geometric defects in high-dimensional embeddings make retrieval inherently vulnerable. Our experiments show that malicious vectors appear in up to 99.85% of top-10 results. Additionally, we evaluate existing hubness mitigation methods as potential defenses against the Black-Hole Attack. The results show that these methods either significantly reduce retrieval accuracy or provide limited protection, which indicates the need for more robust defenses against the Black-Hole Attack.

</details>


### [126] [AttnDiff: Attention-based Differential Fingerprinting for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05502)
*Haobo Zhang,Zhenhua Xu,Junxian Li,Shangfeng Sheng,Dezhang Kong,Meng Han*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AttnDiff：一种基于注意力差异的白盒框架，通过分析模型内在信息路由行为来提取指纹，用于检测大语言模型是否源自特定原始模型，即使经过微调、剪枝或模型合并等操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保护开源大语言模型的知识产权需要验证嫌疑模型是否源自受害者模型，即使经过常见的清洗操作（如微调、剪枝/压缩、模型合并等）。现有方法难以应对这些操作，需要一种能够捕捉模型内在结构特征的方法。

Method: AttnDiff框架通过以下步骤提取模型指纹：1）使用最小编辑的提示对来诱导受控的语义冲突；2）捕获差异化的注意力模式；3）使用紧凑的谱描述符进行总结；4）通过CKA（中心核对齐）比较模型。该方法仅需5-60个多领域探针即可实现高效验证。

Result: 在Llama-2/3、Qwen2.5（3B-14B）及其他开源模型家族上的实验表明，AttnDiff能够高精度区分相关衍生模型与无关模型家族（例如>0.98 vs. <0.22相似度，使用M=60个探针）。该方法在少量探针下即可支持实际的来源验证和问责。

Conclusion: AttnDiff通过分析模型内在的信息路由行为，提供了一种数据高效的白盒框架，能够有效检测大语言模型的衍生关系，即使经过各种清洗操作，为开源模型的知识产权保护提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Protecting the intellectual property of open-weight large language models (LLMs) requires verifying whether a suspect model is derived from a victim model despite common laundering operations such as fine-tuning (including PPO/DPO), pruning/compression, and model merging. We propose \textsc{AttnDiff}, a data-efficient white-box framework that extracts fingerprints from models via intrinsic information-routing behavior. \textsc{AttnDiff} probes minimally edited prompt pairs that induce controlled semantic conflicts, captures differential attention patterns, summarizes them with compact spectral descriptors, and compares models using CKA. Across Llama-2/3 and Qwen2.5 (3B--14B) and additional open-source families, it yields high similarity for related derivatives while separating unrelated model families (e.g., $>0.98$ vs.\ $<0.22$ with $M=60$ probes). With 5--60 multi-domain probes, it supports practical provenance verification and accountability.

</details>


### [127] [Understanding User Privacy Perceptions of GenAI Smartphones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05571)
*Ran Jin,Liu Wang,Shidong Pan,Luona Xu,Tianming Liu,Haoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 用户对GenAI智能手机的隐私担忧及设计建议研究：通过访谈和焦点小组探讨用户对原生集成生成式AI的智能手机的隐私认知、担忧及隐私增强建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GenAI智能手机在系统层面原生嵌入生成式AI，通过持续访问敏感上下文数据实现强大功能，但其隐私风险远超传统移动设备。目前缺乏用户对此类设备隐私影响的认知及期望保障措施的研究，这在GenAI智能手机早期采用阶段尤为关键。

Method: 采用定性研究方法：1) 对22名普通移动用户进行半结构化访谈，探讨他们对GenAI智能手机的使用、隐私担忧和隐私设计期望；2) 后续进行焦点小组讨论，参与者讨论隐私增强建议。

Result: 研究发现：1) 用户在使用GenAI智能手机时对其运作机制理解有限；2) 一旦了解技术细节后，用户表现出更高的隐私担忧；3) 担忧涵盖整个数据生命周期（非透明收集、不安全存储、弱数据控制）；4) 焦点小组提出了跨系统级控制、数据管理实践和用户透明度的协调性隐私增强建议。

Conclusion: 用户的担忧和建议为设计平衡功能与隐私保护的GenAI智能手机提供了以用户为中心的指导，为系统设计者和监管者提供了有价值的启示。研究强调了在GenAI智能手机设计中需要协调系统级控制、数据管理和用户透明度的综合隐私保护方法。

Abstract: GenAI smartphones, which natively embed generative AI at the system level, are transforming mobile interactions by automating a wide range of tasks and executing UI actions on behalf of users. Their superior capabilities rely on continuous access to sensitive and context-rich data, raising privacy concerns that surpass those of traditional mobile devices. Yet, little is known about how users perceive the privacy implications of such devices or what safeguards they expect, which is especially critical at this early stage of GenAI smartphone adoption. To address this gap, we conduct 22 semi-structured interviews with everyday mobile users to explore their usage of GenAI smartphones, privacy concerns, and privacy design expectations. Our findings show that users engage with GenAI smartphones with limited understanding of how these systems operate to deliver functions, but show heightened privacy concerns once exposed to the technical details. Participants' concerns span the entire data lifecycle, including nontransparent collection, insecure storage, and weak data control. In a follow-up focus group, participants discuss a range of privacy-enhancing suggestions that call for coordinated changes across system-level controls, data management practices, and user-facing transparency. Their concerns and suggestions offer user-centered guidances for designing GenAI smartphones that balance functionality with privacy protection, offering valuable takeaways for system designers and regulators.

</details>


### [128] [T2T: Captioning Smartphone Activities Using Mobile Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05642)
*Jiyu Liu,Yong Huang,Yanzhao Lu,Yun Tie,Wanqing Tu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: T2T系统通过加密移动流量生成智能手机活动文本描述，解决了流量特征与活动描述间的语义鸿沟和缺乏文本标注数据的问题，在真实环境中实现了高质量的活动描述生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统智能手机活动分类方法在模型可扩展性和输出可读性方面存在局限。本文旨在通过加密移动流量生成用户活动的文本描述，解决流量特征与活动描述之间的语义鸿沟以及缺乏文本标注流量数据两大挑战。

Method: 提出T2T（Traffic-to-Text）系统，包含流量特征编码器和描述解码器。通过将同步屏幕录制视频输入Qwen-VL-Max视觉语言模型实现移动流量的自动文本标注，并提出多阶段损失函数进行有效的跨模型训练。

Result: 在包含8名用户和20个移动应用的2个真实环境中收集的40,000个流量-描述对上进行评估，T2T获得BLEU-4得分58.1、METEOR得分38.3、ROUGE-L得分70.5、CIDEr得分108.7。定量和定性分析表明T2T能生成语义准确的描述，性能与视觉语言模型相当。

Conclusion: T2T系统成功实现了通过加密移动流量生成智能手机活动文本描述的目标，在模型可扩展性和输出可读性方面超越了传统活动分类方法，为解决语义鸿沟和缺乏标注数据问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: This paper studies the creation of textual descriptions of user activities and interactions on smartphones. Our approach of referring to encrypted mobile traffic exceeds traditional smartphone activity classification methods in terms of model scalability and output readability. The paper addresses two obstacles to the realization of this idea: the semantic gap between traffic features and smartphone activity captions, and the lack of textually annotated traffic data. To overcome these challenges, we introduce a novel smartphone activity captioning system, called T2T (Traffic-to-Text). T2T consists of a flow feature encoder that converts low-level traffic characteristics into meaningful latent features and a caption decoder to yield readable transcripts of smartphone activities. In addition, T2T achieves the automatic textual annotation of mobile traffic by feeding synchronized screen capture videos into the Qwen-VL-Max vision-language model, and proposing multi-stage losses for effective cross-model training. We evaluate T2T on 40,000 traffic-description pairs collected in two real-world environments, involving 8 smartphone users and 20 mobile apps. T2T achieves a BLEU-4 score of 58.1, a METEOR score of 38.3, a ROUGE-L score of 70.5, and a CIDEr score of 108.7. The quantitative and qualitative analyses show that T2T can generate semantically accurate captions that are comparable to the vision-language model.

</details>


### [129] [Stealthy and Adjustable Text-Guided Backdoor Attacks on Multimodal Pretrained Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05809)
*Yiyang Zhang,Chaojian Yu,Ziming Hong,Yuanjie Shao,Qinmu Peng,Tongliang Liu,Xinge You*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出文本引导后门攻击(TGB)，使用文本描述中的常见词作为后门触发器，结合视觉对抗扰动实现可控攻击，在多模态预训练模型上验证其实用性和隐蔽性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门攻击依赖视觉或多模态触发器，在实际数据中很少出现，缺乏实用性。需要开发更隐蔽、实用的攻击方法揭示多模态预训练模型的安全漏洞。

Method: 提出文本引导后门攻击(TGB)：1) 使用文本描述中的常见词作为后门触发器；2) 在中毒样本上引入视觉对抗扰动，调节模型对文本触发器的学习；3) 实现可控可调节的攻击。

Result: 在基于多模态预训练模型的下游任务(组合图像检索CIR和视觉问答VQA)上验证，TGB在不同现实场景中实现了实用性和隐蔽性，攻击成功率可调节。

Conclusion: TGB攻击揭示了多模态预训练模型的关键安全漏洞，使用常见文本词作为触发器显著提高了隐蔽性和实用性，为模型安全评估提供了新视角。

Abstract: Multimodal pretrained models are vulnerable to backdoor attacks, yet most existing methods rely on visual or multimodal triggers, which are impractical since visually embedded triggers rarely occur in real-world data. To overcome this limitation, we propose a novel Text-Guided Backdoor (TGB) attack on multimodal pretrained models, where commonly occurring words in textual descriptions serve as backdoor triggers, significantly improving stealthiness and practicality. Furthermore, we introduce visual adversarial perturbations on poisoned samples to modulate the model's learning of textual triggers, enabling a controllable and adjustable TGB attack. Extensive experiments on downstream tasks built upon multimodal pretrained models, including Composed Image Retrieval (CIR) and Visual Question Answering (VQA), demonstrate that TGB achieves practicality and stealthiness with adjustable attack success rates across diverse realistic settings, revealing critical security vulnerabilities in multimodal pretrained models.

</details>


### [130] [Inertial Mining: Equilibrium Implementation of the Bitcoin Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06092)
*Manuel Mueller-Frank,Minghao Pan,Omer Tamuz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种名为"惯性挖矿"的新协议，解决比特币自私挖矿问题，使诚实行为成为均衡策略


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比特币挖矿协议存在自私挖矿等有利可图的偏离策略，这破坏了协议的均衡性，需要设计新的协议使诚实行为成为均衡

Method: 提出惯性挖矿协议，该协议只在发生分叉时改变矿工行为，不需要修改比特币共识机制或区块链架构

Result: 惯性挖矿使诚实行为成为均衡策略（假设没有矿工控制超过一半算力），自私挖矿等偏离策略不再有利可图

Conclusion: 惯性挖矿协议解决了比特币的自私挖矿问题，实现了中本聪预期的单最长链结果，且无需改变现有比特币架构

Abstract: The value of proof-of-work cryptocurrencies critically depends on miners having incentives to follow the protocol. However, the Bitcoin mining protocol proposed by Nakamoto (2008) and implemented in practice is well known not to constitute an equilibrium: Eyal and Sirer (2018) construct a profitable deviation called ``selfish mining'' which relies on strategically delaying disclosure of newly mined blocks rather than publishing them immediately. We propose inertial mining, a novel mining protocol. When miners follow inertial mining, they produce the outcome intended by Nakamoto, i.e., a single longest chain. But unlike the Bitcoin mining protocol, inertial mining constitutes an equilibrium (assuming no miner controls more than half of the mining power). Indeed, neither selfish mining nor any other deviation is profitable. Furthermore, inertial mining only changes miners' behavior in the event of off-path forks, and can be implemented in Bitcoin without any changes to its consensus mechanism or blockchain architecture.

</details>


### [131] [LLM4CodeRE: Generative AI for Code Decompilation Analysis and Reverse Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06095)
*Hamed Jelodar,Samita Bai,Tochukwu Emmanuel Nwankwo,Parisa Hamedi,Mohammad Meymani,Roozbeh Razavi-Far,Ali A. Ghorbani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: LLM4CodeRE：一个用于双向代码逆向工程的领域自适应LLM框架，支持汇编到源代码的反编译和源代码到汇编的翻译


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶意软件逆向工程中的代码反编译分析面临挑战，主要由于广泛使用的复杂混淆技术。现有的大语言模型方法通常依赖通用代码预训练，缺乏对恶意软件的适应性。

Method: 提出LLM4CodeRE框架，采用两种互补的微调策略：1）多适配器方法用于任务特定的句法和语义对齐；2）序列到序列统一方法使用任务条件前缀来强制执行端到端生成约束。

Result: 实验结果表明，LLM4CodeRE在性能上超越了现有的反编译工具和通用代码模型，实现了鲁棒的双向泛化能力。

Conclusion: LLM4CodeRE是一个有效的领域自适应LLM框架，能够显著提升恶意软件逆向工程中的代码反编译分析能力，支持双向代码翻译任务。

Abstract: Code decompilation analysis is a fundamental yet challenging task in malware reverse engineering, particularly due to the pervasive use of sophisticated obfuscation techniques. Although recent large language models (LLMs) have shown promise in translating low-level representations into high-level source code, most existing approaches rely on generic code pretraining and lack adaptation to malicious software. We propose LLM4CodeRE, a domain-adaptive LLM framework for bidirectional code reverse engineering that supports both assembly-to-source decompilation and source-to-assembly translation within a unified model. To enable effective task adaptation, we introduce two complementary fine-tuning strategies: (i) a Multi-Adapter approach for task-specific syntactic and semantic alignment, and (ii) a Seq2Seq Unified approach using task-conditioned prefixes to enforce end-to-end generation constraints. Experimental results demonstrate that LLM4CodeRE outperforms existing decompilation tools and general-purpose code models, achieving robust bidirectional generalization.

</details>


### [132] [Signature Placement in Post-Quantum TLS Certificate Hierarchies: An Experimental Study of ML-DSA and SLH-DSA in TLS 1.3 Authentication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06100)
*José Luis Delgado Jiménez*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TLS 1.3后量子迁移不是简单的算法替换问题，而是证书层次设计、链暴露和认证过程中密码成本集中的系统性问题。研究发现SLH-DSA放在服务器叶证书时会导致握手延迟和服务器计算成本数量级增加，而将其限制在上层信任层、在交互叶层使用ML-DSA的策略则保持在实际可行范围内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文的动机是纠正对TLS 1.3后量子迁移的简化理解。传统观点将其视为简单的签名算法替换问题，仅通过原语级基准测试推断部署成本。作者认为在实际的证书认证中，签名家族的实际效果取决于其在证书层次结构中的位置、握手过程中暴露的层次范围以及密码负担在客户端和服务器角色间的分布方式。

Method: 采用基于OpenSSL 3和oqsprovider的本地实验研究方法。建立可复现的实验室环境，比较ML-DSA和SLH-DSA在不同证书放置位置、层次深度和密钥交换模式下的表现。测试配置包括经典、混合和纯后量子配置，特别关注证书层次结构设计对性能的影响。

Result: 最显著的性能断层出现在SLH-DSA放置在服务器叶证书时：握手延迟和服务器端计算成本增加数个数量级。将SLH-DSA限制在上层信任层、在交互叶层保留ML-DSA的策略则保持在实际可行范围内。研究还表明，仅传输大小无法解释这种重负载状态：一旦SLH-DSA到达叶证书，服务器端密码成本成为主导因素。

Conclusion: 后量子TLS迁移应被视为证书层次设计、链暴露和实时认证过程中密码成本集中的系统性问题来评估。最优策略需要仔细考虑签名算法在证书层次中的放置位置，避免将计算密集型的后量子算法放在交互频繁的叶证书层，而是将其限制在上层信任层。

Abstract: Post-quantum migration in TLS 1.3 should not be understood as a flat substitution problem in which one signature algorithm is replaced by another and deployment cost is inferred directly from primitive-level benchmarks. In certificate-based authentication, the practical effect of a signature family depends on where it appears in the certification hierarchy, how much of that hierarchy is exposed during the handshake, and how cryptographic burden is distributed across client and server roles.
  This paper presents a local experimental study of TLS 1.3 authentication strategies built on OpenSSL 3 and oqsprovider. Using a reproducible laboratory, it compares ML-DSA and SLH-DSA across multiple certificate placements, hierarchy depths, and key-exchange modes, including classical, hybrid, and pure post-quantum configurations.
  The clearest discontinuity appears when SLH-DSA is placed in the server leaf certificate. In that configuration, handshake latency and server-side compute cost increase by orders of magnitude, while strategies that confine SLH-DSA to upper trust layers and preserve ML-DSA in the interactive leaf remain within a substantially more plausible operational range. The results further show that transport size alone does not explain the heavy regime: once SLH-DSA reaches the leaf, server-side cryptographic cost becomes dominant.
  The paper argues that post-quantum TLS migration is best evaluated as a problem of certificate-hierarchy design, chain exposure, and cryptographic cost concentration during live authentication.

</details>
